Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the milky_way framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 372. The resulting operator achieves bound O(736/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the milky_way space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 372 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 736. QED.

Each subproblem has a solution by the stability criterion fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
